definition{
  name: icfAppendEntities,
  parameters: [front : (List IcfEntity), back : (List IcfEntity)],
  result: (List IcfEntity),
  body: (match front
    | List.nil => back
    | List.cons item rest => (cons item (icfAppendEntities rest back))),
  recursive_argument: front
}
